  CODE
  {
    VAR
      "--- dynamic commerce ---"@1000000002 : Integer;
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebforms@1000000003 : Codeunit 5447306;
      WebformType@1000000004 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon,Marketplace,SalesPerson';
      Text5447300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Verk�uferbild gel�scht;ENU=Salesperson image deleted';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    BEGIN
      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateSalesperson(Rec);
      GET(Code);
      COMMIT;

      WebshopWebforms.SetWebformType(WebformType::SalesPerson);
      WebshopWebforms.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebforms.ShowWebform;

      GET(Code);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (0):
        FORM ACTIONS (3):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          E-Commerce                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Send Login Data         Zugangsdaten versenden          Action
          Webshop Update Salesperson      Verk�ufer Aktualisieren         Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (2):

          NAME                          DATATYPE        SUBTYPE                       LENGTH

          WebshopManagement             Codeunit        "Webshop Management"
          WebshopUpdateManagement       Codeunit        "Webshop Update Management"
          WebshopWebforms               Codeunit        "Webshop Webforms"
          WebformType                   Option


        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (1):

          NAME

          EditContent


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }
}

                   MESSAGE(Text003,GETFILTER("No."));
                   SETRANGE("No.");
                   RecordFound := FIND(Which);

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      "--- dynamic commerce ---"@1000000002 : Integer;
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;

    PROCEDURE GetSelectionFilter@2() : Code[80];
    VAR
      Cust@1000 : Record 18;
      FirstCust@1001 : Code[30];
      LastCust@1002 : Code[30];
      SelectionFilter@1003 : Code[250];
      CustCount@1004 : Integer;
      More@1005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Cust);
      CustCount := Cust.COUNT;
      IF CustCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Cust.FIND('-');
        WHILE CustCount > 0 DO BEGIN
          CustCount := CustCount - 1;
          Cust.MARKEDONLY(FALSE);
          FirstCust := Cust."No.";
          LastCust := FirstCust;
          More := (CustCount > 0);
          WHILE More DO
            IF Cust.NEXT = 0 THEN
              More := FALSE
            ELSE
              IF NOT Cust.MARK THEN
                More := FALSE
              ELSE BEGIN
                LastCust := Cust."No.";
                CustCount := CustCount - 1;
                IF CustCount = 0 THEN
                  More := FALSE;
              END;
          IF SelectionFilter <> '' THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + '|';
          IF FirstCust = LastCust THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstCust
          ELSE
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstCust + '..' + LastCust;
          IF CustCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
            Cust.MARKEDONLY(TRUE);
            Cust.NEXT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(SelectionFilter);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSelection@1(VAR Cust@1000 : Record 18);
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Cust);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (0):
        PAGE ACTIONS (6):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          E-Commerce                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Send Login Data         Zugangsdaten versenden          Action
          Webshop Update Customer         Online Aktualisieren            Action
          Webshop Open Customer Login     Kundenzugang �ffnen             Action
          Page Webshop Permissions Group  Gruppenberechtigungen           Action
          Page Webshop Permissions Group  Artikelberechtigungen           Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (2):

          NAME                          DATATYPE        SUBTYPE                       LENGTH

          WebshopManagement             Codeunit        "Webshop Management"
          WebshopUpdateManagement       Codeunit        "Webshop Update Management"


        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE EnablePlanningControls@1();
    VAR
      PlanningGetParam@1000 : Codeunit 99000855;
      ReorderCycleEnabled@1010 : Boolean;
      SafetyLeadTimeEnabled@1009 : Boolean;
      SafetyStockQtyEnabled@1008 : Boolean;
      ReorderPointEnabled@1007 : Boolean;
      ReorderQuantityEnabled@1006 : Boolean;
      MaximumInventoryEnabled@1005 : Boolean;
      MinimumOrderQtyEnabled@1004 : Boolean;
      MaximumOrderQtyEnabled@1003 : Boolean;
      OrderMultipleEnabled@1002 : Boolean;
      IncludeInventoryEnabled@1001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      PlanningGetParam.SetUpPlanningControls("Reordering Policy","Include Inventory",
        ReorderCycleEnabled,SafetyLeadTimeEnabled,SafetyStockQtyEnabled,
        ReorderPointEnabled,ReorderQuantityEnabled,MaximumInventoryEnabled,
        MinimumOrderQtyEnabled,MaximumOrderQtyEnabled,OrderMultipleEnabled,IncludeInventoryEnabled);
      CurrForm."Reorder Cycle".ENABLED := ReorderCycleEnabled;
      CurrForm."Safety Lead Time".ENABLED := SafetyLeadTimeEnabled;
      CurrForm."Safety Stock Quantity".ENABLED := SafetyStockQtyEnabled;
      CurrForm."Reorder Point".ENABLED := ReorderPointEnabled;
      CurrForm."Reorder Quantity".ENABLED := ReorderQuantityEnabled;
      CurrForm."Maximum Inventory".ENABLED := MaximumInventoryEnabled;
      CurrForm."Minimum Order Quantity".ENABLED := MinimumOrderQtyEnabled;
      CurrForm."Maximum Order Quantity".ENABLED := MaximumOrderQtyEnabled;
      CurrForm."Order Multiple".ENABLED := OrderMultipleEnabled;
      CurrForm."Include Inventory".ENABLED := IncludeInventoryEnabled;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EnableCostingControls@3();
    BEGIN
      CurrForm."Standard Cost".ENABLED := "Costing Method" = "Costing Method"::Standard;
      CurrForm."Unit Cost".ENABLED := "Costing Method" <> "Costing Method"::Standard;
    END;

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      TblshtgHeader@1000 : Record 5943;
      SkilledResourceList@1001 : Form 6023;
      CalculateStdCost@1005 : Codeunit 5812;
      Text11500@1140000 : TextConst 'DEU=M�chten Sie den neuen Artikel bearbeiten?;ENU=Do you want to edit the new item?';

    PROCEDURE GetSelectionFilter@2() : Code[80];
    VAR
      Item@1000 : Record 27;
      FirstItem@1001 : Code[30];
      LastItem@1002 : Code[30];
      SelectionFilter@1003 : Code[250];
      ItemCount@1004 : Integer;
      More@1005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
      ItemCount := Item.COUNT;
      IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Item.FIND('-');
        WHILE ItemCount > 0 DO BEGIN
          ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
          Item.MARKEDONLY(FALSE);
          FirstItem := Item."No.";
          LastItem := FirstItem;
          More := (ItemCount > 0);
          WHILE More DO
            IF Item.NEXT = 0 THEN
              More := FALSE
            ELSE
              IF NOT Item.MARK THEN
                More := FALSE
              ELSE BEGIN
                LastItem := Item."No.";
                ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
                IF ItemCount = 0 THEN
                  More := FALSE;
              END;
          IF SelectionFilter <> '' THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + '|';
          IF FirstItem = LastItem THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem
          ELSE
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem + '..' + LastItem;
          IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
            Item.MARKEDONLY(TRUE);
            Item.NEXT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(SelectionFilter);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSelection@1(VAR Item@1000 : Record 27);
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (1):

          TYPE            SUBTYPE         SOURCEEXPR                      NAME                            CAPTION

          Field                           "Is Webshop Item"               <Is Webshop Item>               <Webshopartikel>


        PAGE ACTIONS (2):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          <Action26>                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Item                    Webshop Artikel                 Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):
        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }

  {
    VAR
      Text000@1000 : TextConst 'DEU=Diese Funktion kann im Ansichtmodus nicht ausgef�hrt werden.;ENU=Unable to execute this function while in view only mode.';
      SalesSetup@1001 : Record 311;
      ChangeExchangeRate@1002 : Form 511;
      CopySalesDoc@1003 : Report 292;
      DocPrint@1004 : Codeunit 229;
      UserMgt@1005 : Codeunit 5700;
      ArchiveManagement@1007 : Codeunit 5063;
      SalesInfoPaneMgt@1008 : Codeunit 7171;
      SalesHeaderArchive@1006 : Record 5107;

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllowed@1() : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF CurrForm.EDITABLE = FALSE THEN BEGIN
        MESSAGE(Text000);
        EXIT(FALSE);
      END ELSE
        EXIT(TRUE);
    END;

    PROCEDURE ActivateFields@2();
    BEGIN
      CurrForm."Bill-to Customer Template Code".ENABLED("Bill-to Customer No." = '');
      CurrForm."Sell-to Customer Template Code".ENABLED("Sell-to Customer No." = '');
      CurrForm."Sell-to Customer No.".ENABLED("Sell-to Customer Template Code" = '');
      CurrForm."Bill-to Customer No.".ENABLED("Bill-to Customer Template Code" = '');
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateInfoPanel@4();
    VAR
      DifferSellToBillTo@1000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      DifferSellToBillTo := "Sell-to Customer No." <> "Bill-to Customer No.";
      CurrForm.SalesHistoryBtn.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.BillToCommentPict.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.BillToCommentBtn.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.SalesHistoryStn.VISIBLE := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Sell-to Customer No.");
      IF DifferSellToBillTo THEN
        CurrForm.SalesHistoryBtn.VISIBLE := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Bill-to Customer No.")
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ApproveCalcInvDisc@3();
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SalesLines.FORM.ApproveCalcInvDisc;
    END;

    BEGIN
    {

    PROCEDURE UpdateAllowed@1() : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF CurrForm.EDITABLE = FALSE THEN
        ERROR(Text000);
      EXIT(TRUE);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateInfoPanel@2();
    VAR
      DifferSellToBillTo@1000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      DifferSellToBillTo := "Sell-to Customer No." <> "Bill-to Customer No.";
      CurrForm.SalesHistoryBtn.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.BillToCommentPict.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.BillToCommentBtn.VISIBLE := DifferSellToBillTo;
      CurrForm.SalesHistoryStn.VISIBLE := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Sell-to Customer No.");
      IF DifferSellToBillTo THEN
        CurrForm.SalesHistoryBtn.VISIBLE := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Bill-to Customer No.")
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ApproveCalcInvDisc@3();
    BEGIN
      CurrForm.SalesLines.FORM.ApproveCalcInvDisc;
    END;

                                                     OnPush=VAR

                                                              DefaultNumber@1000000004 : Integer;
                                                              i@1000000003 : Integer;
                                                              Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
                                                              OptionString@1000000001 : Text[1024];
                                                              SelectedOption@1000000000 : Integer;
                                                              Text5447300@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Kontakte an Newsletter-System senden;ENU=Send contacts to newsletter-system';
                                                              Text5447301@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Mit welchem Webshop sollen die Kontakte verkn�pft werden?;ENU=With what Shop should the contacts be synced?';
                                                            BEGIN
                                                              //--- dynamic commerce ---
                                                              IF Rec."Webshop Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                                                                WebshopSetup.GET;
                                                                DefaultNumber := 1;

                                                                IF Shop.COUNT > 1 THEN BEGIN
                                                                  i := 1;
                                                                  IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                    IF i <> 1 THEN
                                                                      OptionString += ',';
                                                                    OptionString += Shop.Description;
                                                                    IF Shop.Code = WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" THEN
                                                                      DefaultNumber := i;
                                                                    i += 1;
                                                                  UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                                                                  SelectedOption := DIALOG.STRMENU(OptionString,DefaultNumber,Text5447301);
                                                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                                                  SelectedOption := 1;
                                                                END;
                                                                IF SelectedOption > 0 THEN BEGIN
                                                                  IF SelectedOption = 1 THEN
                                                                    Shop.FINDFIRST
                                                                  ELSE BEGIN
                                                                    Shop.RESET;
                                                                    i := 1;
                                                                    IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                      i += 1;
                                                                    UNTIL (SelectedOption=i) OR (Shop.NEXT = 0);
                                                                  END;
                                                                END;
                                                              END ELSE
                                                                Shop.GET("Webshop Code");

                                                              IF (Rec."Webshop Code" <> '') OR (SelectedOption > 0) THEN BEGIN
                                                                WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorOpen(Text5447300);
                                                                WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,TRUE,Shop);
                                                                WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,FALSE,Shop);
                                                                WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorClose;
                                                              END;
                                                            END;
                                                             }
                                                 }
                                                  }

    VAR
      SegInteractLanguage@1000 : Record 5104;
    BEGIN
      CurrForm."Campaign Target".ENABLED("Campaign No." <> '');
      CurrForm."Campaign Response".ENABLED("Campaign No." <> '');
      CurrForm."Correspondence Type (Default)".ENABLED("Ignore Contact Corres. Type" = TRUE);
      CurrForm."Language Code (Default)".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Subject (Default)".ENABLED(SegInteractLanguage.GET("No.",0,"Language Code (Default)"));
      CurrForm.Attachment.ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Ignore Contact Corres. Type".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Information Flow".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Initiated By".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Unit Cost (LCY)".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Unit Duration (Min.)".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
      CurrForm."Language Code (Default)".ENABLED("Interaction Template Code" <> '');
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (0):
        PAGE ACTIONS (2):

          NAME                                CAPTION                                 TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          E-Commerce                          <E-Commerce>                            ActionGroup

      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.

                                                   { ID=17;
                                                     PushAction=RunObject;
                                                     CaptionML=[DEU=Aufga&ben;
                                                                ENU=T&o-dos];
                                                     RunObject=Form 5096;
                                                     RunFormLinkType=OnUpdate;
                                                     RunFormView=SORTING(Segment No.);
                                                     RunFormLink=Segment No.=FIELD(No.) }
                                                   { ID=1000000000;
                                                     CaptionML=DEU=E-Commerce }
                                                   { ID=1000000001;
                                                     MenuLevel=1;
                                                     CaptionML=[DEU=Kontakte mit E-Commerce Marketing synchronisieren;
                                                                ENU=Synchronize Contacts with E-Commerce Marketing];
                                                     OnPush=VAR
                                                              WebshopSetup@1000000005 : Record 5447300;
                                                              DefaultNumber@1000000004 : Integer;
                                                              i@1000000003 : Integer;
                                                              Shop@1000000002 : Record 5447301;
                                                              OptionString@1000000001 : Text[1024];
                                                              SelectedOption@1000000000 : Integer;
                                                              Text5447300@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Kontakte an Newsletter-System senden;ENU=Send contacts to newsletter-system';
                                                              Text5447301@1000000006 : TextConst 'DEU=Mit welchem Webshop sollen die Kontakte verkn�pft werden?;ENU=With what Shop should the contacts be synced?';
                                                            BEGIN
                                                              //--- dynamic commerce ---
                                                              IF Rec."Webshop Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                                                                WebshopSetup.GET;
                                                                DefaultNumber := 1;

                                                                IF Shop.COUNT > 1 THEN BEGIN
                                                                  i := 1;
                                                                  IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                    IF i <> 1 THEN
                                                                      OptionString += ',';
                                                                    OptionString += Shop.Description;
                                                                    IF Shop.Code = WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" THEN
                                                                      DefaultNumber := i;
                                                                    i += 1;
                                                                  UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                                                                  SelectedOption := DIALOG.STRMENU(OptionString,DefaultNumber,Text5447301);
                                                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                                                  SelectedOption := 1;
                                                                END;
                                                                IF SelectedOption = 1 THEN
                                                                  Shop.FINDFIRST
                                                                ELSE BEGIN
                                                                  Shop.RESET;
                                                                  i := 1;
                                                                  IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                                                    i += 1;
                                                                  UNTIL (SelectedOption=i) OR (Shop.NEXT = 0);
                                                                END;
                                                              END ELSE
                                                                Shop.GET("Webshop Code");

                                                              WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorOpen(Text5447300);
                                                              WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,TRUE,Shop);
                                                              WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,FALSE,Shop);
                                                              WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorClose;
                                                            END;
                                                             }
                                                 }
                                                  }
  }
